# Packs-5: every changed displayed overall (owner decision 5)

Generated by `scripts/packs/ovr-diff.ts` (packs-5, the ratings truth re-rate) against the packs-1 fixture `src/ut/__fixtures__/packs-before/<sport>.json`, which was taken on the untouched base. Regenerate with:

```
PACKS_OUT=<dir> PACKSIM_LABEL=after-packs-5 PS_SPORT=<nba|nfl|soccer> npx vite-node scripts/packs/ovr-diff.ts
```

One row per changed edition id: name and season, id, kind, tier (before → now when it moved), the displayed overall at levels 1–5 before and now, the level-1 change, and whether its 90+ status moved at some level ("crosses"). Ids, owned copies, levels, XP and serials never change. The numbered catalogs (`src/data/numbered-*.json`, `supabase/functions/card-registry/numbered.json`) are frozen and were not regenerated: a copy whose number drops below 90 keeps its serial, and every "crosses" row is on the lead's list in `packs.md`.

Spotlight, GOAT, Landmark, Standout and program editions keep their displayed overall (their rows are counted with Δ 0 and not listed). Soccer has no changed row: its award editions are not re-rated; the Hot Seasons under 80 only left the pack buckets.


## Displayed overall changes, nba (before = the packs-1 fixture)
